## Shared routes (printed steps, cited once here and reused by row)

Shorthand: FD = FileManagerDialog.java, FN = FileManagerNode.java,
VM = FileManagerViewModel.java, AD = FileManagerAdapter.java, SA = FileManagerSpinnerAdapter.java.

**R1 — FileManagerDialog activity, dialog-anchored (NOT launcher-anchored).**
Who starts FileManagerDialog (startActivity site / manifest entry) was NOT printed in this
session, so every route below is anchored at FileManagerDialog's own lifecycle/menu/click
handlers, not at the launcher. Inside the dialog, all steps are printed app code:
`FD:120 onCreate` -> factory `FD:135-140` -> `FD:199` swipe listener, `FD:201` storage list,
`FD:208-214` spinner adapter + selection listener, `FD:240 registerMediaReceiver`;
`FD:273-279 onStart` -> subscribeAlertDialog / subscribeAdapter / curDir callback registration;
`FD:499-516 onOptionsItemSelected` (menu dispatch itself is framework, the calls inside are
printed app code); `FD:430-457 onItemClicked` (adapter click callback, calls inside printed).

**R2 — Node list flow.**
`FD:302-309 subscribeAdapter` subscribes `viewModel.childNodes` to `adapter::submitList`
-> `VM:96-99 refreshCurDirectory` / `VM:101-107 updateCurDir` -> `VM:113-145 getChildItems`
-> `AD:88-94 submitList` sorts with `AD:57-61 directoryFirstCmp` and ListAdapter/DiffUtil
-> `AD:96-111 diffCallback`, `AD:126-157 ViewHolder.bind`. The Rx/ListAdapter dispatch steps
are library code, not printed; the calls inside the printed callback bodies are SOURCE.

**R3 — Anonymous-class mapping (by signature, not order alone).**
$1 = the TextWatcher created at FD:176 (methods before/on/afterTextChanged match the plan
signatures exactly, fresh-verified at FD:176-190); $2 = the AdapterView.OnItemSelectedListener
created at FD:214 (onItemSelected/onNothingSelected, fresh-verified at FD:214-238);
$3 = the Observable.OnPropertyChangedCallback created at FD:311-317 (onPropertyChanged,
fresh-verified). Lambda bodies do not create $ classes, so no lambda interferes with this
numbering, but bytecode was not checked — merger must confirm $ indices.

**confirmed_by convention used here (recorded, not decided as a project convention):**
SOURCE = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source
(including a printed listener body that calls it); INFERRED = the method is reached only
through unprinted framework/library dispatch (DialogFragment/activity lifecycle, view-click
dispatch, Rx/ViewModelProvider dispatch, JVM class initialisation, Spinner/BaseAdapter
dispatch) where the registration or show site is printed. This is the per-row convention,
not the whole-route convention used in some earlier batches; labels are unaffected, only
`confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the whole-route convention.

## Shared routes (printed steps, cited once here and reused by row)

Shorthand: FD = FileManagerDialog.java, SA = FileManagerSpinnerAdapter.java,
VM = FileManagerViewModel.java, FAC = FileManagerViewModelFactory.java,
GD = GoToFolderDialog.java.

**R1 — FileManagerDialog activity, dialog-anchored (NOT launcher-anchored).**
As in batch 103, who starts FileManagerDialog was NOT printed, so routes are anchored at
FileManagerDialog's own handlers: `FD:120 onCreate` (factory :135-140, spinner :208-214),
`FD:273-279 onStart`, `FD:499-516 onOptionsItemSelected`, `FD:430-457 onItemClicked`,
`FD:636-650 reloadSpinner`, plus the $2 spinner callback at FD:214-238 and the $3
curDir callback at FD:311-317. The ViewModel is obtained through the AndroidX
ViewModelProvider library (FD:140); that dispatch step is INFERRED, the factory and
constructor bodies it reaches are printed.

**R2 — Spinner rendering (framework dispatch).**
FD:211 sets the spinner adapter (`binding.storageSpinner.setAdapter(storageAdapter)`).
Android's Spinner/BaseAdapter framework then calls getCount/getView/getDropDownView/
getItemId; that framework code was not printed, so those four rows are INFERRED, while
calls made inside their printed bodies (getStorageItem, getTitle, getStoragePath) are SOURCE.

**R3 — GoToFolderDialog show flow.**
FD:548-555 goToDirectoryDialog() -> GD:55-62 newInstance() (new GoToFolderDialog + Bundle)
-> `show(fm, ...)` (fragment-manager dispatch, INFERRED for the fragment lifecycle itself)
-> GD:91-105 onCreateDialog -> GD:107-126 initLayoutView -> GD:128-145 initAlertDialog,
whose OnShowListener lambda at GD:137-139 wires the positive button to goToDir().

**confirmed_by convention used here (recorded, not decided as a project convention):**
SOURCE = a direct app-to-app call site naming the method is printed in app source
(including a printed listener body that calls it, and an internal call site printed in the
definition file itself where the calling method is itself SOURCE-anchored); INFERRED = the
method is reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch (activity/fragment
lifecycle, ActivityResult/Spinner/BaseAdapter dispatch, ViewModelProvider/Rx dispatch, JVM
class initialisation) where the registration or entry is printed. This is the per-row
convention, not the whole-route convention used in some earlier batches; labels are
unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the
whole-route convention.

## Conventions recorded, not decided

- `confirmed_by` in this file is per-row: SOURCE = a printed direct in-app call site naming
  the method (including a printed listener/field body that calls it, and, for row 30,
  the printed field initializer in LogViewModel :107-110 executed on LogViewModel construction,
  itself SOURCE-anchored at LogActivity :92); INFERRED = reached only through unprinted
  framework/library dispatch (activity/fragment lifecycle, DialogFragment lifecycle,
  preference-change dispatch, Rx/databinding dispatch, JVM class initialisation) where the
  registration, show, or entry site is printed. Labels are unaffected; only `confirmed_by`
  would change at merge under a whole-route INFERRED convention.
- **Fragment-attach caveat (rows 5, 6, 9, 10, and, by dispatch, 8, 11):** LogSettingsActivity
  sets `activity_log_settings` as content view (:42), but the step attaching LogSettingsFragment
  to that activity (layout fragment declaration / transaction) was NOT printed in this session.
  The fragment lifecycle rows are therefore INFERRED for the framework dispatch step, with the
  in-app registration/activity-start sites printed. Under a strict rule requiring a printed
  attach step, those rows revert to OPEN — the merger must pick one strict-entry/attach
  convention for all batches. This caveat does not change any label below.
- Lambda numbering is by source order / registration order, not bytecode-verified; labels
  are unaffected by any numbering swap within a group.

## Conventions recorded, not decided

- `confirmed_by` in this file is per-row: SOURCE = a printed direct in-app call site naming
  the method; INFERRED = reached only through unprinted framework/library dispatch
  (activity lifecycle, menu lifecycle, view-click dispatch, RecyclerView/adapter-library
  dispatch, Rx dispatch, JVM class initialisation) where the registration, show, entry, or
  method-reference site is printed. Labels are unaffected; only `confirmed_by` would change
  at merge under a whole-route INFERRED convention.
- **Strict-entry:** the launcher entry IS printed (manifest :43, MAIN :53 / LAUNCHER :54), so
  there is no strict-entry caveat for MainActivity.onCreate itself. Framework dispatch steps
  for lifecycle/callback rows are still INFERRED where the framework, not app code, performs
  the call.
- **FLAGGED — batch 108 row 30, `MainActivity.onFragmentFinished` (see table):** this row rests
  ONLY on the FragmentCallback contract. No printed in-app fragment caller of
  `MainActivity.onFragmentFinished` was found in any file freshly printed in this session
  (MainActivity.java, SessionLogFilterDialog.java, LogActivity.java, AndroidManifest.xml),
  and no `search_code` result was used to search for one. It is kept REACHABLE proposed
  INFERRED here on the contract (MainActivity `implements FragmentCallback` :98, body
  :806-811 handling DetailTorrentFragment + two-pane → `torrentDetailsClosed`), following
  the contract-only treatment used for sibling FragmentCallback rows. **It reverts to OPEN if
  the merger requires a printed fragment caller** — the merger must pick one convention for
  all FragmentCallback rows (cf. batch 99 row 2, batch 107/108 sibling rows, batch 114).
  This flag does not decide that convention, and it does not change any other row.

## confirmed_by convention used here (recorded, not decided)

Per-row immediate-caller rule, as in the prior proposal: SOURCE = the method is called directly by printed app code (a direct call, or a call written in a printed method/lambda body). INFERRED = the method is only invoked by framework/library dispatch with no printed app call site (Android lifecycle, listener interfaces, Rx operator emission, widget dispatch, JVM class initialisation). This differs from the whole-route INFERRED convention used in some other batches; labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the whole-route convention. That convention choice is UNDECIDED and is not decided here.

## CONDITIONAL flip table — OPEN-closing round 2 (rows 2–4 only; flip all three together or none)

Convention dependency: AndroidX preference-inflation ctor convention (xi).
Printed premises already established (not decisions): each 1-arg ctor body only
delegates to the 2-arg ctor (Summary :39-42, SwitchBar :40-43, Time :32-36);
all three classes reach the app only through XML inflation — pref_anonymous_mode
declares SwitchBarPreference (:3) and SummaryPreference (:8) and is loaded by
AnonymousModeSettingsFragment.java:67, pref_scheduling declares TimePreference
(:11, :26) and is loaded by SchedulingSettingsFragment.java:166; round-2 grep
finds zero programmatic 1-arg constructions and only `findPreference` uses.
The 2-arg ctors of the same classes are judged REACHABLE INFERRED in batch 115
on the same XML premise — that does NOT transfer to the 1-arg rows.

Printed dispatch lead (recorded, NOT adopted as the androidx print): the
support-v7 predecessor `android.support.v7.preference.PreferenceInflater`
(fetched 2026-11-01, `https://www.javatips.net/api/android-sdk-sources-for-api-level-23-master/android/support/v7/preference/PreferenceInflater.java`)
has exactly one constructor signature and no fallback:
`CONSTRUCTOR_SIGNATURE = new Class[] { Context.class, AttributeSet.class }`,
`mConstructorArgs = new Object[2]` (`args[0] = mContext`, `args[1] = attrs`),
`createItem` does `clazz.getConstructor(CONSTRUCTOR_SIGNATURE)` then
`constructor.newInstance(args)`, and fully-qualified tag names (the app's three
custom preferences are fully-qualified tags) take the same `createItem` path.
Corroboration, not dispatch: the androidx Preference API reference states the
2-arg ctor is "Constructor that is called when inflating a Preference from
XML" and the 1-arg ctor is "Constructor to create a Preference."

## confirmed_by convention used here (recorded, not decided)

Per-row immediate-caller rule, as in batch 109: SOURCE = the method is called directly by printed app code (a direct call, or a call written in a printed method/lambda body). INFERRED = the method is only invoked by framework/library dispatch with no printed app call site (Android lifecycle, listener interfaces, Rx operator emission, widget/SelectionTracker/ActivityResult dispatch, JVM class initialisation). This differs from the whole-route INFERRED convention used in some other batches; labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the whole-route convention. That convention choice is UNDECIDED and is not decided here.

## Conventions used (recorded, NOT decided)

- SOURCE = a printed app-code line names the method, including a printed call site
  inside another app method body, a method reference (`TorrentListItem::new`), or a
  printed construction site. INFERRED = execution depends on an unprinted dispatch
  step (framework/library callback invocation, ViewModelProvider instantiation, Rx
  operator invoking a lambda, JVM class initialisation), even though the surrounding
  app code is printed. Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, SOURCE rows crossing
  unprinted dispatch would read INFERRED; labels are unaffected.
- Strict-entry: NARROWED, not removed, by this pass. Freshly printed: manifest
  MainActivity block (AndroidManifest.xml:43, MAIN/LAUNCHER :53-55) and
  MainActivity.onCreate :182 `setContentView(R.layout.activity_main)` plus :184-185
  `findFragmentById(R.id.main_fragmentContainer)` recovering mainFragment. The only
  step NOT printed is the `activity_main.xml` layout declaration placing MainFragment
  in that container. Under a strict rule requiring that printed layout step, the
  MainFragment-anchored rows revert to OPEN; under the lifecycle/container convention
  used by sibling batches, all stand. The prior proposal's broader caveat (launcher
  step not printed) no longer applies.
- MainFragment$3 identity: anonymous classes in MainFragment.java in source order
  are $1 DefaultItemAnimator (:142), $2 SelectionObserver (:166), $3
  ActionMode.Callback (:457). So plan rows `MainFragment$3.*` are the
  ActionMode.Callback methods at MainFragment.java:457-498 (lines 460, 466, 475, 494).
  Numbering is source-order, NOT bytecode-verified.
- MainViewModel lambda numbering ($0 searchFilter field lambda :60-67, $1 getFilter
  lambda :118-123, $2 deleteTag runnable :187) follows the proposal's mapping and is
  NOT bytecode-verified; labels do not depend on it (each body sits on a printed route).
- `<clinit>` on first use (convention ix): row 40 rests on the non-constant static
  `diffCallback` field (TorrentListAdapter.java:308) plus first use via
  `new TorrentListAdapter(this)` at MainFragment.java:136. Whether that suffices
  project-wide is undecided; the row reverts to OPEN if the merger requires more.

## Shared routes (printed steps, cited once and reused by row)

All file references below are to the prefix above unless stated otherwise. DEA = `ui/main/drawer/DrawerExpandableAdapter.java`, DG = `ui/main/drawer/DrawerGroup.java`, DGI = `ui/main/drawer/DrawerGroupItem.java`, ET = `ui/main/drawer/EmptyTagItem.java`, NT = `ui/main/drawer/NoTagsItem.java`, TI = `ui/main/drawer/TagItem.java`, TA = `ui/main/drawer/TagsAdapter.java`, MA = `ui/main/MainActivity.java`.

**R-DRAWER — MainActivity drawer.** MA.initLayout -> initDrawer :230-293. initDrawer :248-250: `Utils.getNavigationDrawerItems(this, ...)` -> `new DrawerExpandableAdapter(groups, drawerItemManager, this::onDrawerItemSelected)` :250 -> `drawerItemManager.createWrappedAdapter(drawerAdapter)` :251 -> onDrawerGroupsCreated() :252 -> drawerItemsList.setAdapter(wrappedDrawerAdapter) :255 -> drawerItemManager.attachRecyclerView(drawerItemsList) :259. The wrapped adapter / manager / RecyclerView then dispatch the expandable-adapter callbacks (library dispatch, INFERRED, not printed from library source).

**R-TAGS — MainActivity tags list.** Same initDrawer :270-272: tagsList.setLayoutManager, `tagsAdapter = new TagsAdapter(tagsClickListener)` :271, tagsList.setAdapter(tagsAdapter) :272. subscribeTags :470-486: `Flowable.just(new EmptyTagItem())` :475, `Flowable.just(new NoTagsItem())` :476, `Flowable.fromIterable(list).map(TagItem::new)` :477, then `tagsAdapter.submitList(items)` :485 (ListAdapter/DiffUtil dispatch, INFERRED). setInitSelection :490-525 calls tagsAdapter.setSelectedItem with a new EmptyTagItem :498/:509, a new NoTagsItem :501, or a matched TagItem :519. MainActivity:331 calls tagsAdapter.getSelectedItem() inside the tag-menu handler; :334 calls setSelectedItem(emptyItem).

**R-DIFF — TagsAdapter DiffUtil callback (the batch-113 special item).** TagsAdapter:130-147 defines static `diffCallback` (anonymous class $1): areContentsTheSame :133-138 `return oldItem.equals(newItem);`, areItemsTheSame :141-146 `return oldItem.isSame(newItem);`. DiffUtil runs this callback when submitList is called (R-TAGS :485) — library dispatch, INFERRED. This is the printed route for drawer.TagItem.equals: TagItem.equals is NOT no_trigger_in_app. It is REACHABLE proposed, INFERRED, on this route. The same callback is the second route for every AbstractTagItem subclass's equals/isSame. This contradicts the out/methods.csv UNREACHABLE-unclassified entry for this method; the plan's TODO_CANDIDATE_REACHABLE is the correct direction on this evidence.

**Confirmed_by convention used here.** SOURCE = the method's own call site is printed in app code (explicit `new`, method call, or `super(...)` in a printed constructor, or a printed call inside the same file). INFERRED = the only step into the method is a framework/library dispatch (RecyclerView, ListAdapter/DiffUtil, View click, PopupMenu, JVM class initialisation) or a call inside an unprinted method body. A row whose upstream callback is INFERRED but whose own call site is a printed explicit call is marked SOURCE, with the upstream caveat noted — e.g. holder constructors and typed bind overloads. Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, the SOURCE rows whose upstream dispatch is library/framework would flip to INFERRED; labels are unaffected, only confirmed_by would change at merge.

**Confirmed_by convention used here (same as batch 113).** SOURCE = the method's own call site is printed in app code (explicit `new`, method call, or `super(...)` in a printed constructor, or a printed call inside the same file). INFERRED = the only step into the method is a framework/library dispatch (activity/fragment lifecycle, View/PopupMenu click, LiveData observer, Parcelable marshalling, JVM class initialisation) from a printed registration/start site. A row whose upstream dispatch is INFERRED but whose own call site is a printed explicit call is marked SOURCE, with the upstream caveat noted. Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, those SOURCE rows would flip to INFERRED; labels are unaffected, only confirmed_by would change at merge.

## Counts — proposed, not verified (mechanically verified: 40 rows, sequence 1-40 intact)

- UNREACHABLE proposed: 0
- OPEN: 3 / 40 (rows 22, 23, 27)
- Verified for merge: 0

## Caveats restated (merger must see)

1. Strict-entry caveat: (a) the LAUNCHER -> MainActivity step (AndroidManifest) was NOT freshly re-printed in this pass, so under a strict rule the TagsAdapter holder rows (1-9) and the whole settings trunk (MA:743 -> SA -> SF) revert to OPEN until the manifest + MainActivity.onCreate chain is re-printed at merge; (b) the step attaching SettingsFragment to SettingsActivity (layout or transaction) was NOT printed, so under a strict rule SF rows 33-40 revert to OPEN until activity_settings layout (or a transaction site) is printed. The PreferenceActivity route (R-PREF) does NOT depend on (b): it is anchored at SF.startActivity, which is printed, but it does depend on (a) via MA:743.
2. Two-pane gate: PA.onCreate finishes immediately on large-screen devices (PA:58-61), so the PreferenceActivity fragment route (rows 12, 15, 17, 26) runs only on small screens; on large screens the equivalent route is SF.setFragment (row 40). Both branches are printed, so this is a configuration branch, not an OPEN row — but a device check on a large-screen config would not exercise rows 12/15/17/26.
3. No row is verified for merge: no build, sampler, grep, or C1 check was run (no local checkout). A merge step must re-derive every row from a full checkout, run the C1 checks, print the manifest + activity_settings attachment, confirm lambda/anonymous numbering (rows 3-5, 9, 25, 30, 35) against bytecode, run a saved full-repo grep for rows 22/23/27, and spot-check at least 1 in 5 citations. Build/C1: not run (Mac after push).
4. SettingsFragment.newInstance (SF:63-70) and SettingsViewModel are NOT batch rows and are not judged here; newInstance is cited only as the internal construction site for row 34.
5. Undecided conventions recorded, not decided: per-row SOURCE vs whole-route INFERRED (see convention note above); strict-entry rule (caveat 1); two-pane gate treatment (caveat 2). Labels are unaffected by the confirmed_by choice.
6. No connector search_code result was used as evidence of absence. No fresh HTTP 429 was hit in this pass (the single fresh fetch this session was Utils.java for batch 113, which succeeded; no fresh fetch was needed for batch 114 beyond the re-reads above).

## Shared routes (printed steps, cited once and reused by row)

**R-SETTINGS — MainActivity menu → SettingsActivity → SettingsFragment → section fragments.** MainActivity settings-menu → SettingsActivity and the launcher → MainActivity step were printed in sibling batches (114 and 109–110) but were NOT freshly printed in this pass — see Caveats. Fresh in this pass: SettingsActivity.onCreate :42–62 (ViewModelProvider :47, setContentView(R.layout.activity_settings) :49, detailTitle observer :58–61); both settings layouts host SettingsFragment (portrait + two-pane, above); SettingsFragment.onViewCreated :82–129 (ViewModelProvider :88, two-pane default Appearance fragment :94–95, click listeners on every section preference :98–128); SettingsFragment.openPreference :136–212 (large-screen setFragment(newInstance()) vs small-screen startActivity(class) per section); SettingsFragment.setFragment :220–230 (writes viewModel.detailTitleChanged :222, replaces detail_fragment_container :225–228); SettingsFragment.startActivity :232–241 (builds the PreferenceActivity intent + config parcel :235–239, startActivity :240); PreferenceActivity.onCreate :52–83 (small-screen guard :58–61 finishes on large screens; reads the config parcel :68–71; setFragment(getFragment(fragment)) :82); PreferenceActivity.getFragment :94–122 (dispatches each section simple name back to its newInstance()).

**Confirmed_by convention used here.** SOURCE = the method's own call site is printed in app code (explicit `new`, method call, or a printed call inside the same file). INFERRED = the only step into the method is a framework/library dispatch (fragment/activity lifecycle, preference inflation, widget/listener dispatch, Rx subscription, ViewModelProvider instantiation, JVM class initialisation) from a printed registration/start site. A row whose upstream dispatch is INFERRED but whose own call site is a printed explicit call is marked SOURCE, with the upstream caveat noted. Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, those SOURCE rows would flip to INFERRED; labels are unaffected, only confirmed_by would change at merge.

## Caveats the merger must see (recorded, NOT decided)

1. **Strict-entry:** the entry chain is now printed from SettingsActivity.onCreate through the settings layouts to SettingsFragment and on to every section fragment (see R-SETTINGS). What was NOT freshly printed in *this* pass is launcher → MainActivity and MainActivity settings-menu → SettingsActivity (printed in sibling batches 109–110 and 114, not fresh here). Under a rule requiring every step freshly printed in this pass, all REACHABLE rows revert to OPEN; under the trunk convention used by sibling batches, all stand.
2. **confirmed_by:** per-row convention (see above). Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, SOURCE rows crossing unprinted Rx/ActivityResult/framework dispatch would read INFERRED; labels unaffected.
3. **Preference-inflation ctor (convention (xi) family):** AndroidX dispatch itself was NOT printed (no fw_src/AndroidX print in this pass), so the 2-arg ctor rows are INFERRED. But they are robust under either inflation hypothesis: each class's 1-arg ctor delegates to its 2-arg ctor (SummaryPreference :39–42, SwitchBarPreference :40–43, TimePreference :32–36), so the 2-arg ctor runs whether inflation calls the 1-arg or the 2-arg ctor, and the 3-/4-arg ctors follow by printed delegation. This is the mirror of batch 11 rows 2–4, where the 1-arg ctors stay OPEN precisely because inflation may use the 2-arg ctor instead — do not flip batch 11 on this file's reasoning without re-reading batch 11.
4. **<clinit> on first use (convention (ix)):** rows resting on non-constant `TAG = ...getSimpleName()` plus a printed first use (batch 115 row 29; batch 116 rows 8, 23, 30) are INFERRED and revert to OPEN if the merger requires more than first-use evidence.
5. **Lambda/anonymous numbering:** source-order / enclosing-method mapping only, NOT bytecode-verified (batch 115 rows 39–40; batch 116 rows 1, 13, 14, 33–40). Labels do not depend on it except for the descriptor match noted per row (e.g. batch 115 row 40's descriptor includes the captured SettingsRepository, matching the initLegacy lambda).
6. **API-27 gate (batch 115 rows 38–40):** these are call-site-gated, not body-early-return: the only call site of initLegacyNotifySettings never runs at API 27, and both lambdas' only triggers sit inside it. Under the method-vs-branch rule they are closed_by_api_level candidates. Contrast batch 44–46, where the same Appearance/TorrentNotifier gates were used for ledIndicator getters — consistent treatment, but the merger must apply one rule to all of them. The onPreferenceChange legacy branches (Appearance :240–250) are likewise listener-less at API 27, but onPreferenceChange itself is reachable via the bound preferences, so that row stays REACHABLE.
7. **SummaryPreference.setSummary rows (batch 115 rows 7–8) stay OPEN** on a printed-corpus check only: no caller in any freshly fetched file, and the override dereferences summaryView (null until onBindViewHolder), which argues the XML summary attribute is not routed through the override. A saved full-repo grep is still owed before any flip to UNREACHABLE; a grep hit flips them to REACHABLE.

## Shared routes (printed steps, cited once and reused by row)

**R-SETTINGS — MainActivity menu → SettingsActivity → SettingsFragment → section fragments.** MainActivity settings-menu → SettingsActivity and the launcher → MainActivity step were printed in sibling batches (114 and 109–110) but were NOT freshly printed in this pass — see Caveats. Fresh in this pass: SettingsActivity.onCreate :42–62 (ViewModelProvider :47, setContentView(R.layout.activity_settings) :49, detailTitle observer :58–61); both settings layouts host SettingsFragment (portrait + two-pane, above); SettingsFragment.onViewCreated :82–129 (ViewModelProvider :88, two-pane default Appearance fragment :94–95, click listeners on every section preference :98–128); SettingsFragment.openPreference :136–212 (large-screen setFragment(newInstance()) vs small-screen startActivity(class) per section); SettingsFragment.setFragment :220–230 (writes viewModel.detailTitleChanged :222, replaces detail_fragment_container :225–228); SettingsFragment.startActivity :232–241 (builds the PreferenceActivity intent + config parcel :235–239, startActivity :240); PreferenceActivity.onCreate :52–83 (small-screen guard :58–61 finishes on large screens; reads the config parcel :68–71; setFragment(getFragment(fragment)) :82); PreferenceActivity.getFragment :94–122 (dispatches each section simple name back to its newInstance()).

**Confirmed_by convention used here.** SOURCE = the method's own call site is printed in app code (explicit `new`, method call, or a printed call inside the same file). INFERRED = the only step into the method is a framework/library dispatch (fragment/activity lifecycle, preference inflation, widget/listener dispatch, Rx subscription, ViewModelProvider instantiation, JVM class initialisation) from a printed registration/start site. A row whose upstream dispatch is INFERRED but whose own call site is a printed explicit call is marked SOURCE, with the upstream caveat noted. Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, those SOURCE rows would flip to INFERRED; labels are unaffected, only confirmed_by would change at merge.

## Caveats the merger must see (recorded, NOT decided)

1. **Strict-entry:** the entry chain is now printed from SettingsActivity.onCreate through the settings layouts to SettingsFragment and on to every section fragment (see R-SETTINGS). What was NOT freshly printed in *this* pass is launcher → MainActivity and MainActivity settings-menu → SettingsActivity (printed in sibling batches 109–110 and 114, not fresh here). Under a rule requiring every step freshly printed in this pass, all REACHABLE rows revert to OPEN; under the trunk convention used by sibling batches, all stand.
2. **confirmed_by:** per-row convention (see above). Under a whole-route INFERRED convention, SOURCE rows crossing unprinted Rx/ActivityResult/framework dispatch would read INFERRED; labels unaffected.
3. **Preference-inflation ctor (convention (xi) family):** AndroidX dispatch itself was NOT printed (no fw_src/AndroidX print in this pass), so the 2-arg ctor rows are INFERRED. But they are robust under either inflation hypothesis: each class's 1-arg ctor delegates to its 2-arg ctor (SummaryPreference :39–42, SwitchBarPreference :40–43, TimePreference :32–36), so the 2-arg ctor runs whether inflation calls the 1-arg or the 2-arg ctor, and the 3-/4-arg ctors follow by printed delegation. This is the mirror of batch 11 rows 2–4, where the 1-arg ctors stay OPEN precisely because inflation may use the 2-arg ctor instead — do not flip batch 11 on this file's reasoning without re-reading batch 11.
4. **<clinit> on first use (convention (ix)):** rows resting on non-constant `TAG = ...getSimpleName()` plus a printed first use (batch 115 row 29; batch 116 rows 8, 23, 30) are INFERRED and revert to OPEN if the merger requires more than first-use evidence.
5. **Lambda/anonymous numbering:** source-order / enclosing-method mapping only, NOT bytecode-verified (batch 115 rows 39–40; batch 116 rows 1, 13, 14, 33–40). Labels do not depend on it except for the descriptor match noted per row (e.g. batch 115 row 40's descriptor includes the captured SettingsRepository, matching the initLegacy lambda).
6. **API-27 gate (batch 115 rows 38–40):** these are call-site-gated, not body-early-return: the only call site of initLegacyNotifySettings never runs at API 27, and both lambdas' only triggers sit inside it. Under the method-vs-branch rule they are closed_by_api_level candidates. Contrast batch 44–46, where the same Appearance/TorrentNotifier gates were used for ledIndicator getters — consistent treatment, but the merger must apply one rule to all of them. The onPreferenceChange legacy branches (Appearance :240–250) are likewise listener-less at API 27, but onPreferenceChange itself is reachable via the bound preferences, so that row stays REACHABLE.
7. **SummaryPreference.setSummary rows (batch 115 rows 7–8) stay OPEN** on a printed-corpus check only: no caller in any freshly fetched file, and the override dereferences summaryView (null until onBindViewHolder), which argues the XML summary attribute is not routed through the override. A saved full-repo grep is still owed before any flip to UNREACHABLE; a grep hit flips them to REACHABLE.

## confirmed_by convention (stated, so the merger can reconcile it)

- **SOURCE** = at least one direct app-to-app call site for the method is printed in app source (including a printed lambda body calling it).
- **INFERRED** = the method is only reached through a dispatch not printed from app/fw source: DialogFragment/Activity/Fragment lifecycle, RecyclerView, DiffUtil, view-click / key / show listeners, Rx method references and lambdas, `ViewModelProvider.get`, or layout inflation. Framework/library source itself was not printed, so those steps are INFERRED, not SOURCE.
- This is the per-row convention; it differs from whole-route INFERRED used in some earlier batches (e.g. batch 14). Labels are unaffected by the difference; only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the whole-route convention.

## Lambda / anonymous-class numbering (NOT bytecode-verified)

- `STD`: `lambda$onResume$0` = key listener (:87, `(DialogInterface,int,KeyEvent)Z`); `lambda$initAlertDialog$3` = `OnShowListener` (:153, `(DialogInterface)V`, the only DialogInterface lambda in that method — descriptor-pinned); `lambda$initAlertDialog$1` = new-tag button click (:156-158, registered first) and `$2` = cancel button click (:159-161, registered second), both `(View)V` — `$1`/`$2` assignment is by registration order, merger must confirm against bytecode. Note the OnShowListener lambda is registered before the button lambdas in source (:153 vs :156/:159) yet carries the higher number $3; the $1/$2 swap, if wrong, changes no label (both are click lambdas on the same dialog).
  `lambda$subscribeTags$4` = `flatMapSingle` function (:169, `(List)SingleSource`).
- `TA.$1` = the anonymous `DiffUtil.ItemCallback` in the static `diffCallback` field (:65-82); its two methods are the plan rows.
- `TA.ViewHolder.lambda$bind$0` = the only lambda in `bind` (:104-108).
- `TTL`: `lambda$buildChip$0` = close-icon click lambda (:69-73); `lambda$buildAddTagButton$1` = add-tag button click lambda (:80-84). File order (buildChip before buildAddTagButton) matches $0 before $1.

## confirmed_by convention (stated, so the merger can reconcile it)

- **SOURCE** = at least one direct app-to-app call site for the method is printed in app source (including a printed lambda body calling it).
- **INFERRED** = the method is only reached through a dispatch not printed from app/fw source: DialogFragment/Activity/Fragment lifecycle, RecyclerView, DiffUtil, view-click / key / show listeners, Rx method references and lambdas, `ViewModelProvider.get`, or layout inflation. Framework/library source itself was not printed, so those steps are INFERRED, not SOURCE.
- This is the per-row convention; it differs from whole-route INFERRED used in some earlier batches (e.g. batch 14). Labels are unaffected by the difference; only `confirmed_by` would change at merge if the merger adopts the whole-route convention.

Note the OnShowListener lambda is registered before the button lambdas in source (:153 vs :156/:159) yet carries the higher number $3; the $1/$2 swap, if wrong, changes no label (both are click lambdas on the same dialog).
  `lambda$subscribeTags$4` = `flatMapSingle` function (:169, `(List)SingleSource`).
- `TA.$1` = the anonymous `DiffUtil.ItemCallback` in the static `diffCallback` field (:65-82); its two methods are the plan rows.

  method as a direct callee on a printed chain from the TorrentStreamServer anchor (ctor/start/stop/setter chains); INFERRED means

  batch-14 whole-route rule with the batch-15/16 printed-callee rule project-wide; the two rules are NOT silently mixed here.

  preference change (batch 14) and TorrentEngine streaming start (batches 15/16). No launcher -> settings-page and no

## Conventions used in this file (recorded, not decided)

- Status: UNVERIFIED proposal only. NOT verified for merge. No row in this file has passed spot-check, py_compile, build, or C1.
- confirmed_by is only SOURCE or INFERRED, never DYN / FW-MEMORY / UNVERIFIED.
- Batch 14 rule: every REACHABLE row is INFERRED because the route crosses two unprinted framework dispatches (WorkManager
  enqueue -> doWork, and SAX parser.parse -> FeedHandler.startElement). Batch 15/16 rule: SOURCE means a printed line names the
  method as a direct callee on a printed chain from the TorrentStreamServer anchor (ctor/start/stop/setter chains); INFERRED means
  the only printed path crosses Thread.start, a virtual/framework dispatch, or JVM class-load. The merger must reconcile the
  batch-14 whole-route rule with the batch-15/16 printed-callee rule project-wide; the two rules are NOT silently mixed here.
- Strict-entry caveat (undecided, applies to every REACHABLE row in this file): routes are anchored at FeedSettingsFragment
  preference change (batch 14) and TorrentEngine streaming start (batches 15/16). No launcher -> settings-page and no
  launcher -> TorrentService/TorrentEngine step was printed in this pass. Under a strict printed-launcher-entry rule, all
  REACHABLE rows in this file revert to OPEN. Recorded, not decided.
- UNREACHABLE proposed rows are all INFERRED and grep-owed: a saved full-repo grep (rule-F/C5 form) for the method name must be
  run before merge. A saved grep hit for a live caller flips that row to REACHABLE; a grep hit for a trigger of
  BaseItem.equals/hashCode flips batch 14 rows 11/24 to REACHABLE. No connector search_code 0-result was used as absence evidence
  anywhere in this file, and no row was flipped from OPEN to UNREACHABLE on absence alone without a named grep target.
- Plan labels were NOT adopted: every batch 14-16 plan row is TODO_CANDIDATE_REACHABLE with the generator label
  "REACHABLE (generator candidate, no hand route yet)". Verdicts below were re-derived from printed source, not copied from the
  plan, from prior proposals, or from corrected files (those were leads only; where this file agrees with a corrected file, the
  agreement is a check result, not a copy).
- Build: NOT RUN (runs on the user's Mac after a push). C1: NOT RUN. Never claimed passed.

## Per-row judgements

`confirmed_by` here is PER-ROW (SOURCE = a printed direct app-code call site naming the method, including a printed internal call from a REACHABLE method in the same class; INFERRED = unprinted library/Rx/JVM dispatch with registration/subscription printed). Under a WHOLE-ROUTE convention, rows whose route crosses an unprinted dispatch (Rx subscription, library Listener callback, enhanced-for iterator dispatch, JVM enum init) would read INFERRED; labels are unaffected, only `confirmed_by` would change. That convention is recorded, not decided. OPEN rows carry no `confirmed_by` (shown as `—`).

## confirmed_by convention in this file

- `INFERRED` for every row below. For UNREACHABLE-proposed rows it means: the definition
  is printed, the printed app/library caller set is disjoint from the method (or, for
  the NanoHTTPD internal chains, the only printed caller is itself uncalled), and the
  label is a proposal pending the saved full-repo grep. For OPEN rows it means: at best
  an inference, because the source is absent (generated code) — see rows 37–40.
- No row uses `DYN`, `FW-MEMORY`, or `UNVERIFIED` as `confirmed_by`.

interfaces, exactly: `feed.getTitle()` (:86), `feed.getItemList()` (:95),

`item.getEnclosures()` (:170), `enclosure.getUrl()` (:174) / `enclosure.getType()` (:175),
`item.getMediaRss()` (:190), `media.getContent()` (:194), `content.getType()` (:202),
`media.getHash()` (:217), `item.getEzRssTorrentItem()` (:238),
`torrentItem.getInfoHash()` (:242). It does NOT call `feed.getLink()`,
`feed.getPubDate()` (feed-level), `feed.getCategories()`, `feed.getType()`,
`item.getAuthor()`, `item.getCategories()`, `item.getType()`, `media.getPeerLinks()`,
or any `PeerLink` accessor. Traps avoided: `item.getPubDate()` (:104) is item-level and
does not touch feed-level row 2; `enclosure.getType()` (:175) and `content.getType()` (:202)
are different classes and do not touch item-level row 6.

**Streaming server (TorrentStreamServer, fresh).** `TorrentStreamServer extends NanoHTTPD`
(:51). Its ctor calls `super(host, port)` (:69); its `start()` calls `super.start()` (:78);
its `stop()` calls `super.stop()` (:84); its `serve()` calls only multi-arg
`newFixedLengthResponse(...)` (:127, :130, :134, :144, :147, :196, :209, :222, :231, :239,
:244, :256) and, on the session, `getUri()` (:113), `getParms()` (:132), `getHeaders()` (:150).
It never calls `newChunkedResponse`, the single-arg `newFixedLengthResponse`, or any
other NanoHTTPD row in this batch.
